1.4 Document Conventions
• Table names: snake_case, plural where natural (students, audit_log).
• Column names: snake_case, no abbreviations (created_at not crt_at).
• Foreign keys: <entity>_id suffix (school_id, branch_id, student_id).
• Indexes: <table>_<columns>_idx suffix; unique uses _uidx; partial adds _p.
• Constraints: <table>_<purpose>_ck (check), _fk (foreign), _uq (unique).
• Enums: PascalCase singular type name (AttendanceStatus); values UPPER_SNAKE_CASE.
• Primary keys: UUID v7 (time-sortable) — id column.
• Timestamps: timestamptz (with timezone) for all date/time columns; never plain timestamp.
• Money: numeric(12,2) or integer cents — never float.
• Soft delete: deleted_at timestamptz NULL; partial index WHERE deleted_at IS NULL.
• Bilingual content: Marathi/Hindi business context + English technical terms preserved as user
provided.

2. Database Architecture Overview
PreOne हा(cid:2) PostgreSQL 16 वरे build आहा(cid:8). PostgreSQL चा(cid:15) choice खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)ला reasons ने(cid:8) आहा(cid:8): ACID compliance,
native UUID support, jsonb with GIN indexing, native enum types, row-level security (RLS) for multi-
tenancy, partial + expression indexes, point-in-time recovery (PITR), logical replication for read
replicas, आणिण strong ecosystem (Prisma, pgAdmin, psql, pgvector for AI). PostgreSQL 16 च्या(cid:2) UUID v7
support ने(cid:8) time-sortable primary keys णिमळतो(cid:2)तो जे(cid:8) multi-region writes साहा साहाजे handle केरेतो(cid:2)तो.
2.1 Topology
Production topology मध्या (cid:8)पु,मखा(cid:27) components आहा(cid:8)तो: Primary instance (rw), 2 Read replicas (ro — 1 sync,
1 async), PgBouncer connection pooler, WAL archive for PITR, S3 for backups, cross-region replica
(DR).
+-------------------+

| (async replica) |
+---------+---------+
|
| WAL stream
|
+---------+ +-----------+ +-----------+ +--v----------------+
| App +----->+ PgBouncer +----->+ Primary +----->+ WAL Archive (S3) |
| Servers | | (pool) | | (rw) | | PITR source |
+---------+ +-----+-----+ +-----+-----+ +-------------------+

2.2 Multi-Tenancy Model
PreOne हा(cid:2) shared-schema multi-tenant model व(cid:2)पुरेतो(cid:18) — साव/ tenants एकेचा database + schema share
केरेतो(cid:2)तो, पु,त्याके(cid:8) row मध्या(cid:8) school_id column tenant discriminator म्हाणने! के(cid:2)म केरेतो(cid:18). हा(cid:2) model chosen के(cid:2)रेण
tenant count 10,000+ असाला(cid:8) आणिण per-tenant database maintain केरेण (cid:8)operationally expensive हा(cid:18)ईला.
Alternative options considered: schema-per-tenant (rejected — 10k+ schemas degrade catalog
performance), database-per-tenant (rejected — operationally unmanageable at scale). Shared
schema with RLS हा(cid:2) sweet spot आहा(cid:8): query performance good, backup/restore simple, migration
uniform.
Row-Level Security (RLS) policies enforce tenant isolation at database level. Application connections
set school_id via session variable (SET app.school_id = ?); RLS policies automatically filter rows. Cross-
tenant access requires platform_admin role + explicit bypass.
2.3 Connection Pooling
PgBouncer transaction-mode pooling व(cid:2)पुरेतो(cid:18) — 10,000+ concurrent application connections ला(cid:2) ~100
database connections शी(cid:15) multiplex केरेतो(cid:18). Pool size per-tenant cap configured के(cid:8) ला(cid:2) आहा(cid:8) (max 10
concurrent transactions per tenant) ज्या(cid:2)मळ(cid:27) (cid:8) noisy-neighbor problem prevent हा(cid:18)तो(cid:18).
2.4 Backup + DR
Backup strategy: PITR via WAL archiving (5-minute RPO), daily logical backup (pg_dump) per tenant
for granular restore, weekly full cluster snapshot, monthly restore drill. RTO 30 minutes for single-
tenant restore, 2 hours for full cluster restore. Cross-region DR replica maintains async replication;
failover run-book documented separately.

3. Naming Conventions & Standards
Naming conventions हा(cid:8) database maintainability चा(cid:8) foundation आहा(cid:8)तो. PreOne मध्या(cid:8) खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)ला standards
enforce के(cid:8) ला (cid:8)जे(cid:2)तो(cid:2)तो — PR review मध्या (cid:8)convention violations block हा(cid:18)तो(cid:2)तो आणिण automated linter (sqlfluff +
custom rules) CI pipeline मध्या (cid:8)run हा(cid:18)तो(cid:18).
3.1 Naming Standards Reference
Element Convention Rationale
Tables snake_case, plural where natural Plural reads better in JOINs and
(e.g., students, audit_log) avoids keyword clashes
Columns snake_case, no abbreviations Long names acceptable —
(created_at not crt_at) PostgreSQL column names are
cheap
Primary Keys id, type uuid, default UUID v7 enables time-sortable
uuid_generate_v7() keys + multi-region writes
Foreign Keys <entity>_id, e.g., school_id, Suffix _id is mandatory;
branch_id, student_id composite FKs use _oid pair
Indexes <table>_<columns>_idx, e.g., _idx suffix; unique uses _uidx;
students_branch_id_idx partial adds _p suffix
Constraints <table>_<purpose>_ck for check, Explicit naming aids migration
_fk for foreign, _uq for unique review + ROLLBACK

| ------- | -------------------- | -------------------------- |
| Enums   | PascalCase singular  | Stored as native PG enum;  |
|         | (AttendanceStatus,   | values UPPER_SNAKE_CASE    |
InvoiceStatus)
Timestamps timestamptz for all date/time  Never use timestamp without tz
|     | columns | — multi-region breaks it |
| --- | ------- | ------------------------ |
Money numeric(12,2) — never float,  12 digits + 2 decimals covers
|     | never integer paise | INR/USD with safety margin |
| --- | ------------------- | -------------------------- |
Boolean boolean with default false;  Avoids ambiguity with status
|     | column name starts with is_ or  | enums |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ----- |
has_
JSON jsonb for structured config, jsonb  Never use json (text-only) —
|     | GIN index when queried | loses query optimization |
| --- | ---------------------- | ------------------------ |
Soft Delete deleted_at timestamptz NULL;  Restores via UPDATE;
|     | partial index WHERE deleted_at  | permanent delete via admin tool  |
| --- | ------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
|     | IS NULL                         | only                             |
3.2 Examples
Concrete examples खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)लापु,म(cid:2)ण:(cid:8)
-- Tables
CREATE TABLE students (...);
CREATE TABLE audit_log (...);

-- Columns
school_id      UUID NOT NULL,
created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
student_code   varchar(20),

-- Indexes

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX students_student_code_uidx ON students(student_code);
CREATE INDEX students_active_idx           ON students(branch_id)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- Constraints
CONSTRAINT students_status_ck CHECK (status IN
('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','GRADUATED')),
CONSTRAINT students_school_fk FOREIGN KEY (school_id) REFERENCES schools(id)
  ON DELETE RESTRICT

Multi-tenancy हा(cid:8) PreOne च्या(cid:2) architecture चा(cid:2) core decision आहा(cid:8). Shared-schema model ने(cid:8) tenant

6. Indexing Strategy
Indexing हा(cid:8) database performance चा(cid:2) single most important factor आहा(cid:8). PreOne मध्या (cid:8)indexing strategy
documented, deliberate, आणिण measured आहा(cid:8). पु,त्याके(cid:8) index चा(cid:2) specific query pattern serve केरेतो(cid:18) — नेके(cid:18)
तो(cid:8) indexes write performance degrade केरेतो(cid:2)तो आणिण storage waste हा(cid:18)तो(cid:18).
6.1 Index Type Reference
Type When to Use Example
btree Equality + range queries student_id = ?, created_at
(default) BETWEEN ? AND ?
btree_unique Natural key uniqueness student_code, invoice_number
enforcement
gin jsonb containment queries config @> '{"key":"value"}'
gist Geometric + full-text search + tsrange overlap exclusion
range overlaps
brin Time-series tables (append-only, audit_log.occurred_at
naturally ordered)
partial Subset of rows often queried WHERE deleted_at IS NULL,
WHERE is_active = true
expression Function-based lookup lower(email)
composite Multiple columns often queried (school_id, branch_id, status)
together
covering (INCLUDE) Additional columns to avoid Index-only scans for hot paths
heap fetch
6.2 Common Index Patterns in PreOne
• Foreign key columns always indexed — PostgreSQL does NOT auto-index FKs.
• Partial index WHERE deleted_at IS NULL on soft-delete tables — active rows only.
• Partial index WHERE is_active = true on status-flag tables — active records only.
• Composite index (school_id, branch_id, ...) — tenant + scope pruning.
• BRIN on time-series tables (audit_log, sms_log, trip_log) — 90% smaller than btree.
• GIN on jsonb config columns — @> containment queries.
PreOne ERD v3.0 Page 26 of 403

PreOne ERD v3.0  |  Database Schema & Physical Design
•  Unique indexes on natural keys (student_code, invoice_number, phone) — enforce business
uniqueness.
6.3 Index Maintenance
Index bloat monitoring weekly run हा(cid:18)तो(cid:18) — pages_reclaimed आणिण fragmentation ratio track हा(cid:18)तो(cid:18).
REINDEX CONCURRENTLY scheduled monthly for high-write tables (audit_log, sms_log, attendances).
Auto-vacuum tuned per-table — high-write tables get aggressive autovacuum settings; low-write
tables default.

7.2 FK ON DELETE Behaviors
FK ON DELETE behavior हा(cid:2) data integrity चा(cid:2) critical decision आहा(cid:8). PreOne मध्या(cid:8) खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)ला rules enforce
हा(cid:18)तो(cid:2)तो:
PreOne ERD v3.0                                                              Page 27 of 403

PreOne ERD v3.0 | Database Schema & Physical Design
• RESTRICT — parent table deletion blocked (default for school, branch, student, user).
• CASCADE — child rows auto-deleted (for junction tables, e.g., role_permission, user_role).
• SET NULL — child FK set to NULL (for soft references, e.g., approved_by, created_by).
• Hard deletes देणि(cid:27)म/ळ — soft delete (deleted_at) preferred. RESTRICT के(cid:8) वळ schema-migration
safety सा(cid:2)ठी(cid:15).

PreOne मध्या (cid:8)hybrid approach: critical enums (AttendanceStatus, InvoiceStatus) native enum; evolving
enums varchar + CHECK constraint. CHECK constraint faster migrate केरेतो(cid:2) या(cid:8)तो(cid:18).

8.1 Soft Delete Pattern
Hard deletes देणि(cid:27)म/ळ आहा(cid:8)तो — पु,त्या(cid:8)के business table मध्या(cid:8) deleted_at timestamptz NULL column आहा(cid:8). Row
'delete' म्हाणजे(cid:8) UPDATE ... SET deleted_at = now(). Restore म्हाणजे(cid:8) UPDATE ... SET deleted_at = NULL.
Permanent delete हा(cid:2) admin tool द्वा(cid:2)रे(cid:8) manual — देरे permanent delete audit_log मध्या (cid:8)record हा(cid:18)तो(cid:18).
-- Soft delete trigger (simplified)
CREATE TRIGGER soft_delete_before_delete
BEFORE DELETE ON students
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION soft_delete_handler();
CREATE FUNCTION soft_delete_handler() RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
UPDATE students SET deleted_at = now() WHERE id = OLD.id;
RETURN NULL; -- suppress actual DELETE
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

9.2 Encryption at Rest
Confidential + Restricted columns pgcrypto extension ने(cid:8) encrypted store हा(cid:18)तो(cid:2)तो — column-level
encryption. Example: aadhaar_number stored as bytea (pgcrypto symmetric), masked display
column aadhaar_number_masked (XXXX-XXXX-XXXX format) for UI. Database-level TDE (Transparent
Data Encryption) additional layer — disk-level encryption via LUKS for EBS volumes.
-- Example: Aadhaar encryption
ALTER TABLE students ADD COLUMN aadhaar_encrypted bytea;
ALTER TABLE students ADD COLUMN aadhaar_number varchar(12);
-- aadhaar_number stores masked: 'XXXX-XXXX-1234'
-- On write:
UPDATE students SET
aadhaar_encrypted = pgp_sym_encrypt('1234-5678-9012',
current_setting('app.encryption_key')),
aadhaar_number = 'XXXX-XXXX-9012'
WHERE id = ?;

§11 (Right to Access) Data subject access request DSAR endpoint aggregating
student + guardian + attendance
+ finance records
§12 (Right to Correction) Correction + completion Correction request table with

§13 (Right to Erasure) Right to be forgotten Archive + minimize workflow
(Section 8 pattern)

17. Domain: Inventory & Procurement
हा(cid:2) domain stock keeping units, purchase requests, POs, GRN, issue register, returns, damaged items,
आणिण stock audits या(cid:2)चा  (cid:2) complete workflow आहा(cid:8). Reorder rules automated आहा(cid:8)तो; low stock alerts
threshold-based trigger हा(cid:18)तो(cid:2)तो.
17.1 Domain KPIs
या(cid:2) domain चा (cid:8)operational health measure केरेण(cid:2)रे(cid:8) KPIs खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)लापु,म(cid:2)ण (cid:8)आहा(cid:8)तो:
KPI Target / Definition
Stock accuracy ≥98% post-audit
GRN-to-stock lag ≤4 hours
PO cycle time Median ≤3 days
Stockout incidents ≤2 per quarter

19. Domain: Finance
हा(cid:2) domain PreOne चा(cid:2) complete financial operations आहा(cid:8) — fee heads, fee plans, invoices, payments,
receipts, GST, refunds, late fees, penalties, discounts, scholarships, expenses, income, आणिण financial
reports. BRC च्या(cid:2) Financial + Compliance rules या(cid:2) domain मध्या (cid:8)enforce हा(cid:18)तो(cid:2)तो.
19.1 Domain KPIs
या(cid:2) domain चा (cid:8)operational health measure केरेण(cid:2)रे(cid:8) KPIs खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)लापु,म(cid:2)ण (cid:8)आहा(cid:8)तो:
| KPI                          |     | Target / Definition      |     |
| ---------------------------- | --- | ------------------------ | --- |
| DSO (Days Sales Outstanding) |     | ≤15 days                 |     |
| Fee collection rate          |     | ≥95% within due date     |     |
| GST compliance               |     | 100% filings on time     |     |
| Reconciliation accuracy      |     | 100% bank ↔ system match |     |

22. Domain: Transport
हा(cid:2) domain transport routes, stops, student assignments, trips, GPS tracking, आणिण transport fee
management आहा(cid:8). Driver + Vehicle master Administration domain मध्या (cid:8)आहा(cid:8)तो; route operations याथे(cid:8) (cid:8).
22.1 Domain KPIs
या(cid:2) domain चा (cid:8)operational health measure केरेण(cid:2)रे(cid:8) KPIs खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)लापु,म(cid:2)ण (cid:8)आहा(cid:8)तो:
KPI Target / Definition
On-time drop rate ≥95%
Route utilization ≥70% capacity
Transport attendance coverage 100% trips
GPS ping interval ≤30 seconds

27. Prisma Schema Mapping
Prisma हा(cid:2) PreOne चा(cid:2) ORM आहा (cid:8)— TypeScript-first schema definition, type-safe queries, आणिण automatic
migrations. या(cid:2) chapter मध्या(cid:8) database tables → Prisma models चा(cid:8) mapping + standard annotations
document आहा(cid:8)तो.
27.1 Naming Conventions
• Table name (snake_case) → Prisma model (PascalCase): students → Student, audit_log →
AuditLog.
• Column name (snake_case) → Prisma field (camelCase): school_id → schoolId, created_at →
createdAt.
• @@map annotation on model to specify table name.
• @map annotation on field to specify column name.
• @@unique for composite unique constraints.
• @@index for composite indexes.
• Enum types defined at top of schema file; values UPPER_SNAKE_CASE.
PreOne ERD v3.0 Page 390 of 403

PreOne ERD v3.0 | Database Schema & Physical Design
27.2 Sample Prisma Model
Student table चा(cid:8) Prisma model definition खा(cid:2)ला(cid:15)लापु,म(cid:2)ण(cid:8) आहा(cid:8) — या(cid:2)चा pattern साव/ tables सा(cid:2)ठी(cid:15) follow के(cid:8) ला(cid:2)
जे(cid:2)तो(cid:18):
model Student {
id String @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
schoolId String @map("school_id") @db.Uuid
branchId String? @map("branch_id") @db.Uuid
studentCode String @unique @map("student_code")
admissionId String? @map("admission_id") @db.Uuid
firstName String @map("first_name")
lastName String @map("last_name")
dob DateTime @db.Date
gender Gender
bloodGroup BloodGroup? @map("blood_group")
status StudentStatus @default(ACTIVE)
currentBranchId String? @map("current_branch_id") @db.Uuid
currentClassroomId String? @map("current_classroom_id") @db.Uuid
currentProgram ProgramType? @map("current_program")
admissionDate DateTime @db.Date @map("admission_date")
exitDate DateTime? @db.Date @map("exit_date")
exitType ExitType? @map("exit_type")
createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")
deletedAt DateTime? @map("deleted_at")
school School @relation(fields: [schoolId], references: [id], onDelete:
Restrict)
branch Branch? @relation(fields: [branchId], references: [id], onDelete:
SetNull)
admission Admission? @relation(fields: [admissionId], references: [id],
onDelete: SetNull)
currentBranch Branch? @relation("StudentCurrentBranch", fields:
[currentBranchId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
currentClassroom Classroom? @relation(fields: [currentClassroomId], references:
[id], onDelete: SetNull)
attendances Attendance[]
observations Observation[]
invoices Invoice[]
classroomAssignments ClassroomAssignment[]
@@index([branchId, status])
@@index([currentBranchId, status])
@@map("students")
}

28.2 Rollback Plan
देरे wave सा(cid:2)ठी(cid:15) rollback plan documented आहा(cid:8) — schema snapshot before migration, dual-write
window where possible, feature flag to revert to v2 logic. Total rollback window 30 days from W11
cutover. Post-W12, rollback requires data loss (v3-only columns).
28.3 Verification
देरे wave post-deploy verification: (a) row count parity, (b) query performance regression test, (c)
application smoke test suite, (d) audit log completeness check, (e) RLS isolation test (cross-tenant
query must return 0 rows).
29. Backup, Restore & DR
Backup + Restore + Disaster Recovery strategy हा(cid:2) PreOne चा(cid:2) business continuity backbone आहा(cid:8). या(cid:2)
chapter मध्या (cid:8)operational procedures + RPO/RTO targets + drill cadence document आहा(cid:8)तो.

PreOne ERD v3.0 | Database Schema & Physical Design
29.2 Restore Procedures
• Single-row restore: PITR WAL replay to staging → extract row → INSERT into production
(manual review).
• Single-tenant restore: pg_dump from daily backup → restore to staging → extract tenant
rows → INSERT/UPDATE into production.
• Schema migration rollback: Snapshot restore to staging → reverse migration → verify →
promote.
• Primary failure: Promote sync replica → update DNS → restart app servers.
• Region failure: Promote cross-region DR replica → update Route53 → failback after primary
region recovery.
29.3 DR Drill Cadence
Drill Type Frequency Owner Success Criteria
Single-tenant restore Monthly Data Platform Restore complete <30
min, 0 data loss
Full cluster restore Quarterly Data Platform + Ops Restore complete <4
hours, RPO 5 min
Region failover Bi-annual Architecture + Ops Failover complete <2
hours, 0 critical alerts
PITR recovery Monthly DBA Target timestamp
recoverable, 0 data loss

Time-series   tables   (audit_log,   sms_log,   whatsapp_log,   email_log,   trip_log,   attendances,
daily_reports, meal_logs, event_store, broadcast_logs, announcement_recipients, medical_logs) हा(cid:8)
monthly णिके व(cid:2) yearly partitioned आहा(cid:8)तो. PostgreSQL 16 native partitioning (declarative) use के(cid:8) ला (cid:8)जे(cid:2)तो(cid:8). Old
partitions DETACH + archive to cold storage (S3 Glacier) after 12 months; DETACH is non-blocking.

| Term |     | Definition                                       |     |
| ---- | --- | ------------------------------------------------ | --- |
| ACID |     | Atomicity, Consistency, Isolation, Durability —  |     |
transaction guarantees
| BRIN |     | Block Range Index — compact index for naturally- |     |
| ---- | --- | ------------------------------------------------ | --- |
ordered data (time-series)
| CASCADE |     | FK ON DELETE behavior — child rows auto-deleted  |     |
| ------- | --- | ------------------------------------------------ | --- |
with parent
| CDC |     | Change Data Capture — pattern for streaming DB  |     |
| --- | --- | ----------------------------------------------- | --- |
changes to downstream systems
| CHECK Constraint |     | Domain constraint enforcing column value  |     |
| ---------------- | --- | ----------------------------------------- | --- |
conditions
| citext |     | Case-insensitive text type — auto-lowercases for  |     |
| ------ | --- | ------------------------------------------------- | --- |
comparison
| DLT |     | Distributed Ledger Technology — India TRAI  |     |
| --- | --- | ------------------------------------------- | --- |
mandate for SMS sender registration
| DPDP |     | Digital Personal Data Protection Act 2023 (India) |     |
| ---- | --- | ------------------------------------------------- | --- |
| DSAR |     | Data Subject Access Request — DPDP §11 right to   |     |
access
| ETL |     | Extract, Transform, Load — data warehouse  |     |
| --- | --- | ------------------------------------------ | --- |
pipeline
EXCLUDE Constraint Prevents overlapping ranges (e.g., room bookings)
| FK  |     | Foreign Key — referential integrity constraint |     |
| --- | --- | ---------------------------------------------- | --- |
| GIN |     | Generalized Inverted Index — for jsonb, array  |     |
containment queries
PreOne ERD v3.0                                                              Page 398 of 403

PreOne ERD v3.0 | Database Schema & Physical Design
Term Definition
GiST Generalized Search Tree — for geometric, range,
full-text
jsonb Binary JSON — queryable, indexable JSON storage
MRR Monthly Recurring Revenue — SaaS metric
MVCC Multi-Version Concurrency Control — PostgreSQL's
isolation mechanism
NOC No Objection Certificate
Partitioning Splitting large tables into smaller physical pieces
pgcrypto PostgreSQL extension for encryption functions
PITR Point-In-Time Recovery — restore to specific
timestamp via WAL replay
PK Primary Key — row uniqueness
PO Purchase Order
RPO Recovery Point Objective — max acceptable data
loss
RLS Row-Level Security — tenant isolation at DB layer
RTO Recovery Time Objective — max acceptable
downtime
SET NULL FK ON DELETE behavior — child FK set to NULL on
parent delete
SaaS Software as a Service
TDE Transparent Data Encryption — disk-level
encryption
TDS Tax Deducted at Source (India)
TTL Time To Live — auto-expiration
UQ Unique constraint
UUID v7 Time-sortable UUID — preferred for distributed PK
generation
WAL Write-Ahead Log — PostgreSQL's durability
mechanism
